/**
 * ============================================================================
 * SCENE Engine v2
 * Author: Icehellionx
 * Role: Momentum / Distance CNN classifier for RP chatbots.
 *
 * High-level:
 *   - Loads a quantized CNN model from getRawModelData().
 *   - Tokenizes the latest user message (preserving [ROLE_X] tokens).
 *   - Runs a forward pass through:
 *       * Embedding layer
 *       * 1D CNN with kernels [3, 4, 5] + max-pool + ReLU
 *       * Two softmax heads:
 *           - Momentum (advance / retreat)
 *           - Distance (intimate / medium / far)
 *   - Appends a [SCENE]...[/SCENE] line into context.character.scenario
 *     describing the top Momentum and Distance predictions.
 *
 * NOTE: Logic and behavior are preserved exactly from the original version.
 *       Only formatting, comments, and naming/log labels have been cleaned up.
 * ============================================================================
 */

(function () {
    'use strict';

    // ============================================================================
    // MOMENTUM / DISTANCE ENGINE CONFIG
    // ============================================================================
    // DEBUG: when set to 1, logs the SCENE output line to the console.
    var DEBUG = 1;

    // Maximum number of tokens processed by the CNN.
    // Should match trainer max_seq_len (default: 60).
    var MAX_TOKENS = 60;

    // ============================================================================
    // SAFE LOGGING
    // ============================================================================
    // Wrap console.log in a try/catch so environments without a console don't break.
    function safeLog(msg) {
        try {
            if (console && console.log) console.log(msg);
        } catch (e) { }
    }

    // ============================================================================
    // MODEL INIT
    // ============================================================================
    var MODEL = null;             // Holds the fully initialized model once loaded.
    var MODEL_INIT_ERROR = false; // Prevents repeated init attempts after a failure.

    function initModel() {
        // If we already initialized (or failed), skip re-initialization.
        if (MODEL || MODEL_INIT_ERROR) return;

        var raw = "";
        try {
            raw = getRawModelData();
        } catch (e) {
            MODEL_INIT_ERROR = true;
            return;
        }

        if (!raw || raw.length < 10) {
            MODEL_INIT_ERROR = true;
            safeLog("[SCENE] Model: Empty raw data.");
            return;
        }

        var parsed;
        try {
            parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        } catch (e) {
            MODEL_INIT_ERROR = true;
            safeLog("[SCENE] JSON parse error: " + e.message);
            return;
        }

        try {
            var w = parsed.weights || {};
            var s = parsed.scales || {};

            // Dequantize a flat or nested weight/bias array using its scalar scale.
            function deq(vals, scale) {
                if (!vals || scale == null) return null;

                var flat = Array.isArray(vals[0]) ? vals.flat(Infinity) : vals;
                var out = new Float32Array(flat.length);
                for (var i = 0; i < flat.length; i++) {
                    out[i] = flat[i] * scale;
                }
                return out;
            }

            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
            // Convolution layers (c0w/c0b, c1w/c1b, c2w/c2b) with kernel sizes [3,4,5]
            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
            var convs = [];
            var K = [3, 4, 5];

            for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
                var wKey = "c" + i + "w";
                var bKey = "c" + i + "b";
                var weight = deq(w[wKey], s[wKey]);
                var bias = deq(w[bKey], s[bKey]);

                if (!weight || !bias) {
                    throw new Error("Missing weights for conv layer " + i);
                }

                convs.push({
                    w: weight,
                    b: bias,
                    k: K[i]
                });
            }

            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
            // Vocab map
            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
            var vocabMap = {};
            if (Array.isArray(parsed.vocab)) {
                for (var j = 0; j < parsed.vocab.length; j++) {
                    vocabMap[parsed.vocab[j]] = j;
                }
            }

            // Fallback ID for unknown tokens (defaults to 1 if <unk> missing).
            var unkId = (vocabMap["<unk>"] !== undefined) ? vocabMap["<unk>"] : 1;

            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
            // Classification heads:
            //   mom_w / mom_b  -> Momentum (advance / retreat)
            //   dist_w / dist_b -> Distance (intimate / medium / far)
            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
            var momW = deq(w.mom_w || w.rel_w, s.mom_w || s.rel_w);
            var momB = deq(w.mom_b || w.rel_b, s.mom_b || s.rel_b);

            var distW = deq(w.dist_w || w.pow_w, s.dist_w || s.pow_w);
            var distB = deq(w.dist_b || w.pow_b, s.dist_b || s.pow_b);

            if (!momW || !momB || !distW || !distB) {
                throw new Error("Missing classification head weights.");
            }

            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
            // Embedding and meta
            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
            var embedding = deq(w.emb_text || w.emb, s.emb_text || s.emb);
            if (!embedding) throw new Error("Missing embedding table.");

            var embedDim = parsed.embed_dim || (parsed.meta && parsed.meta.embed_dim) || 16;
            var vocabSize = parsed.vocab ? parsed.vocab.length : 0;

            MODEL = {
                vocabMap: vocabMap,
                unkId: unkId,
                vocabSize: vocabSize,
                embedDim: embedDim,
                convs: convs,

                embedding: embedding,

                // Heads
                momW: momW,
                momB: momB,

                distW: distW,
                distB: distB,

                // Label sets
                momentumLabels: parsed.momentum_labels || parsed.relation_labels || [],
                distanceLabels: parsed.distance_labels || parsed.power_labels || [],

                // Class counts
                numMomentum: (parsed.momentum_labels || parsed.relation_labels || []).length,
                numDistance: (parsed.distance_labels || parsed.power_labels || []).length
            };

            safeLog("[SCENE] Init success. Vocab: " + MODEL.vocabSize);
        } catch (e) {
            MODEL_INIT_ERROR = true;
            safeLog("[SCENE] Init failed: " + e.message);
        }
    }

    // ============================================================================
    // TOKENIZER — **NO NORMALIZATION AT ALL**
    // ============================================================================
    // ✔ Keeps CAPITALIZATION  
    // ✔ Keeps PUNCTUATION  
    // ✔ Keeps ALL special tokens  
    // ✔ Does NOT lowercase  
    // ✔ Does NOT strip punctuation  
    function cnn_tokenize(text) {
        if (!text) return [];
        return text
            .split(/\s+/)        // split only on whitespace
            .filter(Boolean)     // remove empty strings
            .slice(0, MAX_TOKENS);
    }

    // ============================================================================
    // CONV1D MAX + RELU
    // ============================================================================
    function conv1d_max_relu(inputCHW, weight, bias, C, L, k) {
        if (!weight || !bias) return new Float32Array(0);

        var O = bias.length;
        var out = new Float32Array(O);
        var pad = (k - 1) >> 1;

        for (var f = 0; f < O; f++) {
            var maxVal = -Infinity;
            var wOff = f * C * k;

            for (var t = 0; t < L; t++) {
                var s = bias[f];

                for (var c = 0; c < C; c++) {
                    var baseInput = c * L;
                    var baseWeight = wOff + c * k;

                    for (var j = 0; j < k; j++) {
                        var idx = t + j - pad;
                        if (idx >= 0 && idx < L) {
                            s += inputCHW[baseInput + idx] * weight[baseWeight + j];
                        }
                    }
                }

                var relu_s = s > 0 ? s : 0;
                if (relu_s > maxVal) maxVal = relu_s;
            }

            out[f] = maxVal;
        }

        return out;
    }

    // ============================================================================
    // FORWARD PASS
    // ============================================================================
    function cnn_predict(text) {
        initModel();
        if (!MODEL) return null;

        var tokens = cnn_tokenize(text);
        var ids = tokens.map(function (t) {
            return (MODEL.vocabMap[t] !== undefined) ? MODEL.vocabMap[t] : MODEL.unkId;
        });

        while (ids.length < MAX_TOKENS) ids.push(0);

        var C = MODEL.embedDim;
        var T = MAX_TOKENS;
        var embedded = new Float32Array(C * T);

        for (var t = 0; t < T; t++) {
            var id = ids[t];
            var off = id * C;
            for (var c = 0; c < C; c++) {
                embedded[c * T + t] = MODEL.embedding[off + c];
            }
        }

        var feats = [];
        var total = 0;

        for (var i = 0; i < MODEL.convs.length; i++) {
            var cv = MODEL.convs[i];
            var res = conv1d_max_relu(embedded, cv.w, cv.b, C, T, cv.k);
            feats.push(res);
            total += res.length;
        }

        var flat = new Float32Array(total);
        var ptr = 0;
        for (var k = 0; k < feats.length; k++) {
            flat.set(feats[k], ptr);
            ptr += feats[k].length;
        }

        function forwardHead(W, B, n) {
            var logits = new Float32Array(n);

            for (var r = 0; r < n; r++) {
                var sum = B[r];
                var base = r * total;
                for (var q = 0; q < total; q++) {
                    sum += W[base + q] * flat[q];
                }
                logits[r] = sum;
            }

            var out = new Float32Array(n);
            var maxL = -Infinity;

            for (var b = 0; b < n; b++) {
                if (logits[b] > maxL) maxL = logits[b];
            }

            var sumExp = 0;
            for (var d = 0; d < n; d++) {
                out[d] = Math.exp(logits[d] - maxL);
                sumExp += out[d];
            }

            for (var e = 0; e < n; e++) {
                out[e] /= sumExp || 1;
            }

            return out;
        }

        return {
            momentum: forwardHead(MODEL.momW, MODEL.momB, MODEL.numMomentum),
            distance: forwardHead(MODEL.distW, MODEL.distB, MODEL.numDistance)
        };
    }

    // ============================================================================
    // INFERENCE LOOP
    // ============================================================================
    if (typeof context !== "object" || !context) return;

    context.character = context.character || {};

    var msgs = (context.chat && context.chat.last_messages) || [];
    var last = msgs.length ? msgs[msgs.length - 1].message : "";

    if (last) {
        try {
            var res = cnn_predict(last);
            if (res && MODEL) {
                function fmtTopTwo(labels, arr) {
                    if (!labels || !labels.length) return "";

                    var pairs = [];
                    for (var i2 = 0; i2 < labels.length; i2++) {
                        pairs.push({ label: labels[i2], prob: arr[i2] });
                    }

                    pairs.sort(function (a, b) { return b.prob - a.prob; });

                    var top = pairs.slice(0, 2);
                    return top.map(function (p) {
                        return p.label + ":" + Math.round(p.prob * 100) + "%";
                    }).join(", ");
                }

                var momentumStr = fmtTopTwo(MODEL.momentumLabels, res.momentum);
                var distanceStr = fmtTopTwo(MODEL.distanceLabels, res.distance);

                var line = "[SCENE]" + momentumStr + " | " + distanceStr + "[/SCENE]";

                if (DEBUG) safeLog(line);

                context.character.scenario += line;
            }
        } catch (err) {
            safeLog("[SCENE Error] " + err.message);
        }
    }

    // ============================================================================
    // PASTE JSON MODEL DATA HERE
    // ============================================================================
    function getRawModelData() {
        return [
            '{"model_type": "cnn_momentum_distance_v1", "vocab": ["<pad>", "<unk>", "the", "to", "their", "a", "s", "them", "and", "of", "[role_c]", "between", "[role_a]", "[role_b]", "feels", "in", "[role_d]", "[role_e]", "from", "air", "quiet", "sits", "like", "prop", "it", "ignore", "difficult", "but", "as", "is", "on", "with", "kitchen", "though", "they", "phone", "table", "while", "small",
